# Noether UI/UX Research — July 2026

**Status:** research document only — no code was changed. Every recommendation is traceable to either audited code (`file:line`) or a cited external source.

**Produced:** 2026-07-05, from staging @ `e2eb410`. Line-number citations are valid as of that commit and will drift.

**How it was made:** a 35-agent research fleet — 13 code auditors (one per product surface plus mobile and accessibility sweeps), 16 web researchers (7 competitor teardowns, 8 thematic best-practice studies, 1 live-site probe), and 6 synthesis agents (4 chapter writers, an executive-summary writer, and a completeness critic whose findings were folded back in). Assembled and edited by Claude Code for Yahya & Mert.

**Fixed inputs (founder decisions, not up for debate in this document):**

- Scope: all user-facing surfaces, including the landing page.
- Brand: the gold-on-dark NØETHER identity is **fixed** — every visual recommendation works within it (contrast, hierarchy, spacing, states, motion), none replaces it.
- Audience: **pro-first** — the trading core must meet Hyperliquid/CEX expectations, with progressive disclosure so Stellar-native newcomers aren't lost.
- Benchmarks: perp DEXs (Hyperliquid, dYdX v4, GMX v2, Drift/Jupiter, Lighter/Paradex/Vertex/edgeX), CEX futures UIs (Binance/Bybit/OKX), and retail-simple apps (Robinhood/Coinbase).

## Executive Summary

### Where Noether stands against the 2026 bar

Noether's contract layer is competitive: advanced orders, cross-margin, tiered fees, a vault ecosystem whose 10% profit share and on-chain-enforced 5% leader stake match Hyperliquid's economics, and an atomic verify-then-trade router that structurally eliminates the keeper-execution gap behind GMX's angriest support threads. The presentation layer sits far behind that engine. Thirteen auditors walking every surface against shipped code (staging, 2026-07-04) logged 11 critical and ~50 high-severity findings plus a 51-entry data-integrity ledger of numbers that are hardcoded, fabricated on failure, or wrong by construction: trade history asserting "Fees −$0.00" on every row, a fee-tier readout quoting Tier-3 traders 2.5× their real rate, funding-rate read failures rendered as a healthy green +0.0000%, a marketplace APY that mathematically cannot go negative, a leaderboard whose default PnL ranking excludes liquidation losses — and, worst of all, liquidations that leave the liquidated trader no history row, toast, or record of any kind.

For the chosen pro-first audience this is the most expensive possible weakness. The benchmarking through-line is that trust failures, not feature gaps, killed or maimed venues in 2024–26 (Hyperliquid's JELLY episode, dYdX's 8-hour halt with frozen liquidations, Drift's $295M loss and rebrand); post-Drift, this cohort diligences data integrity and governance before depositing, and reconciles UI numbers against the chain. The encouraging finding is that Noether already owns the correct house pattern — the vault APR's "Shown once fee revenue is tracked" null-gate — and most ledger entries can converge on it cheaply, because the honest data or the right in-repo pattern usually exists a file away (the contract-error decoder, the toast.promise pattern, CrossMarginBanner's equity formula, WalletModal's bottom-sheet treatment).

Against the 2026 interaction bar — sign-once session trading, gasless execution, push-fed order lifecycle — Noether is categorically behind: every action is a wallet popup plus a ~5-second ledger wait the UI neither narrates nor optimistically bridges; failures surface as raw JSON, silence, or a vanished row; /trade's first paint is a blank SSR error shell until ~549KB of JavaScript hydrates; the "Order Book" panel only ever shows the user's own orders; and the README still points at an NXDOMAIN testnet URL. Meanwhile genuine structural advantages go unclaimed: one oracle price that marks, triggers, and liquidates (no mark-vs-last ambush liquidations), price locked at signature via the router, a faucet that erases the funding cliff every competitor still has, and Jupiter's volumes proving the 3-pair oracle-priced shape needs no apology.

The roadmap therefore sequences honesty before polish. Phase A is a stop-lying sweep (copy, tokens, defaults, one-component state machines); Phase B fixes the two systemic causes — client-side chain scans that should be indexer reads, and the missing transaction-lifecycle layer for a 5-second chain; Phase C bundles contract-coupled work into one migration and adds session keys, pre-liquidation alerting, and the trust/status/legal surfaces real money requires. The gold-on-dark brand needs no change anywhere: every visual fix is opacity, weight, or token discipline.

### Top 10 highest-impact improvements

Ordered by impact-per-effort, trust-critical data-integrity items first. IDs reference the Recommendations Roadmap (S ≈ ≤2 days, M ≈ ≤2 weeks, L = multi-week/contract-coupled); per-finding evidence is in the Current-State Audit.

| # | Improvement | Why it matters | Effort | Where detailed |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Make liquidations visible: parse `position_liquidated`/`cross_liq` into history + toast when a position vanishes | Margin seized with no record anywhere is the single worst trust failure a perp venue can ship | M | B1 (+C2) · Trade audit |
| 2 | Portfolio money truth: add cross-margin balance to Net Worth; never price positions at $0 on failed oracle reads | Two criticals: the money page understates funds by the deposited amount and can paint −100% losses that trigger panic-closes | S | A8, A9 · Portfolio audit |
| 3 | /vault feedback + risk: toasts with decoded errors on deposit/withdraw; render the unused RiskDisclosure at the deposit point | Every LP money action currently succeeds and fails silently; principal risk is effectively undisclosed | S | A6, A7 · Vault audit |
| 4 | Fabricated-number sweep: fees/Net PnL → "—", tier labeled "estimated", no green +0.0000% on failure, label Binance-sourced data, single-source hardcoded values | One caught fake number poisons every honest one for an audience that reconciles against the chain | S–M | A13–A19, A31 · Data-integrity ledger |
| 5 | Vault-factory accounting (V-1): liquid-NAV caveats + leader-position scoping now; per-vault balance tracking + close-membership check at redeploy | Three criticals: NAV collapses on capital deployment, exits forfeit in-flight value, leaders can close other vaults' positions | S (UI) → L (contract) | A20, B18 → C1 |
| 6 | Honest vault APY + sortable marketplace: signed time-weighted returns, no annualizing young vaults, TVL-default sort | The primary allocation metric cannot go negative and is trivially inflatable; the oldest-first grid fails the browsing job | M–L | B17 · Vaults audit |
| 7 | Leaderboard integrity: count liquidation losses, error ≠ "No traders yet", defined metrics, pinned self-row | Default ranking flatters liquidated accounts; outages render fake emptiness; "where am I?" is unanswered | S–M | B2, A21–A23 |
| 8 | Bottom-sheet Modal with max-height + internal scroll (WalletModal's in-repo pattern) | Mobile order entry clips its own submit button — the core money flow dead-ends; one component fixes trade, SL/TP, vault, and feedback modals | S | A1 · Mobile audit |
| 9 | Contrast/zoom tokens: near-black text on gold and on the green/red CTAs; re-enable pinch zoom; opacity floors | Money CTAs fail WCAG (white-on-green 2.28:1; latent white-on-gold primary token); zoom is blocked over 8–10px money data | S | A3–A5 · Accessibility audit |
| 10 | 5s-ledger lifecycle kit, then session keys + atomic TP/SL bundles | Staged progress, optimistic rows, decoded errors, tx links — the most consistent benchmark pattern; sign-once is the defining 2026 pro expectation | M–L → L | B21 → C3, C4 |

### If you only do one week of UX work

- **Fix ui/Modal once (A1):** bottom-sheet posture, max-height, internal scroll — copied from WalletModal — un-clips mobile order entry, SL/TP, vault deposit/withdraw, create-vault, and feedback in one component.
- **Fix the tokens (A3, A4):** `--primary-foreground` to near-black, black text on the green/red trade CTAs (MobileTradeBar already does it), and delete `userScalable`/`maximumScale` so money data can be zoomed.
- **Give /vault feedback and honesty (A6, A7):** toast success/failure with decoded contract errors on deposit/withdraw/trustline; render RiskDisclosure beside the deposit card; fix the stale 0.1% fee copy.
- **Stop mis-stating money on /portfolio (A8, A9):** "—" plus a stale badge instead of $0 prices, and cross-margin balance added to Net Worth via the existing `getCrossMarginBalance` read.
- **Dash out /trade fabrications (A14, A15, A18):** Fees/Net PnL → "—" with a "ships with the next contract deploy" tooltip; fee tier labeled "estimated" and hidden on failed reads; funding read failure → "—", never +0.0000%.

## Methodology

### What was examined

**In-repo audit (13 agents).** One auditor per surface — landing, trade order-entry, trade chart/orderbook/positions, portfolio, protocol vault (LP), vaults marketplace + detail, vault leader manage, referrals, leaderboard, faucet + API keys, and global chrome (nav/wallet/layout/terms/restricted) — plus two cross-cutting sweeps: mobile/responsiveness and accessibility/microcopy. Auditors read the shipping code (`web/app`, `web/components`, `web/lib`, and the contracts where UI claims depend on contract behaviour), judged what a *user* experiences, and were required to cite repo-relative `file:line` evidence for every issue. They were also required to record genuine strengths — this is an audit, not a hit piece.

**External research (16 agents).** Seven competitor teardowns (Hyperliquid; dYdX v4; GMX v2 — Noether's closest structural analogue as an oracle-priced, book-less venue; Drift + Jupiter; the 2024–26 new generation — Lighter, Paradex, Vertex, edgeX; Binance/Bybit/OKX futures; Robinhood/Coinbase retail patterns), eight thematic studies (order entry & leverage, position & liquidation-risk display, onboarding & activation, vault/copy-trading UX, referral/points/leaderboard growth mechanics, dark-UI typography/color/contrast, latency masking & on-chain tx feedback, mobile trading), and one probe of what `noether.exchange` actually serves to crawlers and link-unfurlers. Each researcher ran multiple independent web searches and returned patterns with named sources; each pattern carries a Noether-specific applicability note honoring the real constraints (oracle pricing, ~5s wallet-signed transactions, 3 pairs, fixed brand).

**Synthesis.** Findings were deduplicated, severity-ranked, and written into the chapters below; a completeness critic then checked the chapters against the raw finding index (every critical/high finding must surface in both the current-state chapter and the roadmap, all competitor groups and themes represented, no recommendation violating the fixed brand or the 5-second-ledger reality), and its corrections were folded in.

### Volume

- **291 audit findings**: 11 critical, 63 high, 85 medium, 39 low, plus 93 data-integrity entries (hardcoded/fake/placeholder values — tracked separately because fake numbers on a trading product are trust-critical).
- **223 externally-sourced patterns** with Noether-specific applicability notes.
- **~400 cited sources** (deduplicated in the appendix).

### Severity scale

| Severity | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **critical** | Misleads users about money/risk, or blocks a core flow |
| **high** | Materially hurts trust, conversion, or usability |
| **medium** | Real friction or polish gap |
| **low** | Nit |

### Limits of this method

This is a code-level and evidence-based heuristic evaluation plus competitive research — it is **not** a moderated usability test, and it does not replace watching five real traders attempt a first trade (a protocol for that is in [How to Validate](#how-to-validate--metrics--user-testing)). Findings about rendered pixels (contrast ratios, layout at 390px) are computed from the code's tokens and classes rather than screenshots, so a handful may be off where runtime state diverges. Competitor observations reflect public material as of early July 2026.

## Current-State Audit — Page by Page

This chapter consolidates the findings of 13 code auditors who walked every product surface against the shipped code (branch `staging`, 2026-07-04). Every claim carries a repo `file:line` citation. Issues are ordered by severity (Critical → Low); findings reported by multiple auditors appear once, in the most relevant section, with the strongest evidence. Two cross-cutting sections (mobile & responsiveness, accessibility & microcopy) absorb app-wide patterns so per-surface tables stay focused. The chapter closes with a consolidated **data-integrity ledger** — every hardcoded, fake, placeholder, or wrong-by-construction number found anywhere in the product. All recommendations respect the fixed gold-on-dark brand: the fixes below are about contrast, hierarchy, states, and honesty, never about replacing the identity.

---

### Landing page (/)

**Purpose & flow.** The marketing home converts visitors into traders (/trade) and LPs (/vault) while establishing the Emmy-Noether gold-on-dark identity. The trade CTA is one click from the hero; everything else — the full pitch, the LP CTA, and the only faucet link — sits behind 7 hijacked wheel/swipe gestures, each gated by a 1000–1200ms input lockout (`web/components/landing/SlideContainer.tsx:84,90`), ≈8–10s forced minimum to read the page. A `?ref=CODE` referral banner mounts on this route from the root layout and handles the bind transaction well.

**Strengths**

- Hero passes the 5-second test: product, pairs, leverage, and chain named immediately (`web/components/landing/Hero.tsx:16-28`); one-click CTA to /trade from hero and navbar (`Hero.tsx:32-40`, `Navbar.tsx:85-92`).
- Real perf engineering landed: HeroBlobs canvas idles unless hovered with reduced-motion bail (`HeroBlobs.tsx:31-33,108-126,164`); stellar-wallets-kit fully dynamic-imported (`web/lib/stellar/walletKit.ts:1-21`); stellar-sdk lazy-loads only on referral bind (`ReferralBanner.tsx:70-73`) — the June '26 464KB/56%-SDK finding is substantially addressed.
- No fabricated stats on the mounted page: CountUp exists but is unused (`animations.tsx:230`); VaultSection sells a flow, not invented TVL/APY.
- ReferralBanner is a model wallet-tx micro-flow: busy/disabled state, contract errors #10/#11/#12 mapped to plain English, honest "activates when fee accrual ships in v1.1" copy (`ReferralBanner.tsx:62-91,124`).
- Distinctive brand execution (the theorem line, `FlagshipSection.tsx:118-123`); SEO/meta baseline done (`web/app/layout.tsx:17-37`); a real, current /terms page exists (`web/app/terms/page.tsx`); a reduced-motion CSS layer exists (`landing.css:180-190`).

| Severity | Issue | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| High | **Stale, visibly broken product screenshot** — the page's only proof-of-product shows "-9519.75%" 24h change, Turkish "Şub" chart axis, deprecated "Fee (0.1%)", "Gas (XLM) 9,996.60" (contradicting the adjacent "No gas overhead" card), and the pre-T2 nav. Pros zoom into exactly this image. | `web/public/images/trading-screenshot.png`; rendered at `Hero.tsx:64-69`, `FlagshipSection.tsx:54-59`; contradicted claim `FlagshipSection.tsx:14` | Recapture from the current build (en-US locale, seeded realistic data); add a release-checklist item to refresh it when nav/fees change. |
| High | **Scroll hijacking with 1–1.2s lockouts fights reading**: all wheel/touch/key input preventDefaulted, ~8–10s minimum to see the page, trackpad inertia causes slide-skips, no progress indicator (the scroll cue CSS is unused), and the JS controller ignores prefers-reduced-motion. | `SlideContainer.tsx:84,90,120-126,133-136,145-153`; `landing.css:152-159,180-190` | Native scroll + `scroll-snap-type: proximity`, or keep controller with delta accumulation, slide dots, and a matchMedia reduced-motion bypass. |
| High | **Terms/Privacy footer links go to `#`** on a leveraged-trading product, even though a real /terms page exists ("Last updated: July 4, 2026"); no privacy page exists at all. | `LandingFooter.tsx:75-80` vs `web/app/terms/page.tsx` | Point Terms at /terms today (one line); ship a minimal /privacy; surface a one-line jurisdiction notice. |
| High | **Faucet/Portfolio/Vault exiled to 11px links at ~1.4:1 contrast; no testnet onboarding path** — a new user clicking "Start Trading" has zero USDC and the landing never mentions the faucet; the old 4-step onboarding section is unmounted dead code. | `Navbar.tsx:7-13`; `LandingFooter.tsx:72,86-94` (text-white/15, 11px); `FinalSection.tsx:8-13` unmounted per `web/app/page.tsx:14-23` | Add a "Testnet — get free USDC" pill near the hero CTA or restore a compact onboarding strip; raise footer links to legible contrast; consider Faucet in the navbar until mainnet. |
| High | **Navbar/body text fails WCAG AA in both themes**: desktop nav rests at `#eab308/50` (~3.2:1) and `black/50` (~3.9:1); mobile menu gold/60 marginal; light-slide card copy `black/50` fails; footer white/15 ~1.4:1. Fixable entirely within brand — it is resting opacity, not hue. | `Navbar.tsx:76,122`; `FlagshipSection.tsx:91`; `LandingFooter.tsx:72` | Raise resting states to ~/80–90 equivalents (full `#eab308` or a warmed light-gold; black/75 on light); footer to white/60+. |
| High | **Short viewports strand content — including the only "Deposit Now" /vault CTA**: sections are min-height 100dvh with native scroll disabled; VaultSection's ~850px+ mobile stack and FlagshipSection's stack overflow ≤750px viewports and the bottom strip can never be scrolled into view. | `landing.css:23-37`; `VaultSection.tsx:81,126-145`; `FlagshipSection.tsx:174-199`; `SlideContainer.tsx:49-54,133-136` | Fall back to native scrolling on touch/short viewports, or shrink mobile layouts to guaranteed-fit budgets; verify at 375×667. |
| Medium | **~1.9MB unoptimized static assets**: 240KB favicon.svg fetched by every page, 241KB wordmark SVG for a 24px logo, 549KB hero PNG referenced twice with `priority`, ~884KB of autoplaying webm with no posters/preload — largely cancels the JS lazy-loading wins. | `web/public` sizes; `NoetherLogo.tsx:12`; `LandingFooter.tsx:44-51`; `Hero.tsx:64-69` + `FlagshipSection.tsx:54-59`; `VaultSection.tsx:88-95` | SVGO the wordmark (<10KB), small ICO/PNG favicon set, drop below-fold `priority`, add posters + `preload="metadata"`, pause video under reduced motion. |
| Medium | **Hero headline invisible until JS hydrates**: SSR HTML carries `opacity:0` via framer `initial="hidden"`, so the brand message paints seconds after LCP on mid-tier mobile. | `animations.tsx:31-43,147-158`; `Hero.tsx:17-31` | Render above-the-fold text visible in SSR; animate via CSS keyframe or transform-only motion. |
| Medium | **Global key handler breaks keyboard support**: Space/arrows preventDefaulted on `document` without target checks (Space on a focused button scrolls the deck); tabbing desyncs `sectionIdx`; hamburger lacks `aria-expanded`. (Focus-ring color is covered in the accessibility section.) | `SlideContainer.tsx:145-153,25-27,49-54`; `Navbar.tsx:95-99` | Ignore key events when `e.target` is interactive; resync index from scroll/focus; add `aria-expanded`. |
| Medium | **"Vaults" vs "Vault" vs "Deposit Now" — two products share one word**: nav "Vaults" → marketplace (/vaults), the Earn slide's CTA → LP pool (/vault), footer lists "Vault". Scent breaks at the moment of highest intent. | `Navbar.tsx:9`; `VaultSection.tsx:142`; `LandingFooter.tsx:86-94` | Rename destinations distinctly ("Earn"/"Liquidity" for LP pool, "Trader Vaults" for marketplace) and use identical labels everywhere. |
| Medium | **Value-prop content hidden behind scroll phases**: four feature cards sit at opacity:0 until a second scroll (skimmers never see the claims); NOEIntro spends three locked gestures on one sentence; feature icons are imported but never rendered. | `FlagshipSection.tsx:113,133-141,158-168,85-93`; `NOEIntroSection.tsx:18-46` | Show cards readable at phase 0 (use phases for emphasis, not existence), render the icons, collapse NOEIntro to one phase. |
| Medium | **Bare-glyph OG card; no robots/sitemap**: og-image.png is the slashed-O alone (no wordmark/tagline/URL); no `robots.ts`, `sitemap.ts`, or `public/robots.txt` exist. | `web/public/og-image.png`; `web/app/layout.tsx:25-36`; ls confirms absence | Compose a 1200×630 card (wordmark + one-line value prop + domain); add Next metadata-route robots/sitemap. |
| Low | **Docs link is a Google Drive folder; Discord invite is an expirable code** — off-brand for a "verifiable by anyone" pitch; the invite can silently die. | `LandingFooter.tsx:81-83,59` | Host docs on GitHub/docs subdomain; switch to a permanent/vanity invite. |
| Low | **Dead landing code confuses maintenance**: LightPillar (sole three.js consumer), ShinyText, SpotlightCard unused; FinalSection (fake-success email form) and CommunitySection exported but unmounted; CountUp and the scroll-cue CSS unused. | `web/components/LightPillar.jsx:2`; `landing/index.ts:7-8` vs `web/app/page.tsx:14-23`; `FinalSection.tsx:20-24`; `landing.css:152-159` | Delete the dead components; remount a compacted FinalSection with a real email sink (its onboarding steps are wanted) or delete it. |

### Trade page (/trade)

**Purpose & flow.** The money page: pick a market, read the Noeracle mark, place Market/Limit/Stop-Limit/Trailing-Stop orders long or short with isolated or cross margin via a collateral+leverage input model, and manage positions/orders/history — every action a wallet-signed Soroban transaction with ~5s ledger latency. A returning connected trader can market-open in 2 clicks (percent pill → Buy/Long) with defaults from `tradeStore.ts:13-18`; TP/SL can attach at open as pipelined follow-up signatures (`OrderPanel.tsx:252-293,447-451`). Mobile gets a fixed Long/Short bar that presets direction and opens the panel in a sheet (`MobileTradeBar.tsx:28-68`).

**Strengths**

- Price-source integrity: the panel quotes the same ~500ms Noeracle stream the contract executes and liquidates against, with Binance only as a bootstrap fallback, and the reasoning documented in code (`OrderPanel.tsx:30-37,78-87,125-145`); a 10s watchdog plus visible amber "Live prices stale" badge communicates degradation (`web/lib/stellar/noeracle.ts:96-223`, `page.tsx:582-586`).
- Isolated liq preview is formula-identical to on-chain math including the 1% maintenance margin (`web/lib/utils/format.ts:127-141` vs `contracts/noether_common/src/math.rs:38-64`).
- Full tx-lifecycle handling for 5s ledgers: `toast.promise` on open/SL/TP/cancel, double-submit guards everywhere (`OrderPanel.tsx:123,1234`; `PositionsList.tsx:113-126`), staggered 0/2.5/6s read-your-writes refetch (`page.tsx:211-218`), and 60s order-poll diffing that toasts fills/cancels (`page.tsx:230-259`).
- Known contract footguns honestly fenced: cross-margin SL/TP blocked with "contract fix pending" copy (`OrderPanel.tsx:119-121,221-222`); unsupported leader order types elided, not broken (`OrderPanel.tsx:64-71,691-703`).
- SL/TP modals beat most testnet DEXes: quick-set % chips, ROE-based PnL preview at trigger, direction-aware validation, slippage presets (`PositionsList.tsx:337-523`). Orders tab surfaces pro metadata: TIF badges, reduce-only tag, stop-limit phase, live distance-to-trigger (`OrdersList.tsx:142-160,242-274`).
- Market Info OI and 24h volume are now real indexer values with an honest "—" on failure — the old hardcoded $1.2M/$890K are gone (`page.tsx:106-122,656-688`; `web/lib/api/markets.ts:26-37`). Cross positions get an account-level health banner rather than the fake isolated-formula liq price (`CrossMarginBanner.tsx:37-102`) — though a computed cross-liq *estimate* is still owed (see issues).
- Trade History separates Gross PnL / Fees / Net PnL and links every row to stellar.expert (`TradeHistory.tsx:107-111,211-226`); every panel has designed loading skeletons and empty states.

| Severity | Issue | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| Critical | **Liquidations are invisible to the liquidated user** — history parses only `position_closed` on both the Horizon and events paths; `position_liquidated`/`cross_liq` are never queried, and no toast fires when the 60s poll drops a liquidated row. A trader whose margin is seized finds no record: no liq price, no keeper fee, nothing (this also blanks /portfolio history). The only "Liq" render is the unattributed Recent Trades feed within ~14h. | `web/lib/stellar/market.ts:350-405,445,541-554,1019-1032`; `contracts/market/src/lib.rs:526-529` (event exists); `page.tsx:286-300` (no detection) | Parse `position_liquidated` + `cross_liq` for the connected trader into history as "Liquidated" rows (price, keeper reward); diff open-position ids across polls and toast "Position #N liquidated at $X". |
| High | **Order Summary fee/tier/14d volume are fabricated for any trader with prior volume**: `getTraderFeeInfo` calls a `get_trader_volume` view that no longer exists (removed for WASM size), silently catches to 0, then accumulates client-side and resets on refresh. A Tier-3 trader is shown "Base" and a 0.050% fee — 2.5× what the contract charges — and tier progress perpetually restarts. | `web/lib/stellar/market.ts:1272-1288`; `contracts/market/src/lib.rs:639` (removal note; only `storage.rs:572` helper remains); `OrderPanel.tsx:113-114,148-163,182-209,404,428` | Serve 14d volume from the indexer (per-trader endpoint) or restore a tiny view on the next WASM refit; until then label rows "estimated" and hide tier when the read fails. |
| High | **Liq/Entry previews use the current mark for Limit and Stop-Limit orders**: liq price is always computed from `assetPrice`, so a 10x dip-buy shows a liq ABOVE the actual entry; the Stop-Limit summary row is labeled "Entry Price" but displays the mark, ignoring both user inputs. | `OrderPanel.tsx:171-177,1150-1163,1166-1183`; contract computes from fill price at `contracts/market/src/lib.rs:326,344-353` | Base the preview on effective entry (trigger for Limit, limit price for Stop-Limit, mark for Market); relabel the Stop-Limit row and show "--" until entered. |
| High | **Cross mode displays an isolated-formula liq price that does not exist on-chain** — the contract stores `liquidation_price = 0` for cross positions; liquidation is account-level. Traders size risk off a trigger that will never fire. | `OrderPanel.tsx:171-177,1166-1183` vs `contracts/market/src/lib.rs:344-345` | Show a computed account-level estimate in Cross mode — "Liq. (est., cross)" derived from CrossMarginBanner’s existing equity/maintenance aggregates, with a tooltip stating the frozen-other-positions assumption (the every-venue convention; see the Patterns chapter) — never the fake isolated figure. |
| High | **Hidden order flags leak across tabs**: TIF, Reduce-Only, and slippage are single component states whose controls render only under the Limit tab, but Stop-Limit submits encode them and Trailing-Stop/TP-SL reuse the slippage. A Reduce-Only toggled while exploring Limit silently makes a later Stop-Limit entry unfillable, escrowing collateral with zero indication. | `OrderPanel.tsx:90,110-111,939-991,332,341,314-315,260-281,996-1035` | Scope flags per order type (reset on tab switch) or render the controls on every tab that encodes them; echo all active flags in the Order Summary. |
| High | **Unrealized PnL / Net Value omit accrued funding** — PnL is price-only while the contract deducts cumulative funding at close; funding appears nowhere per-position and the close modal shows no "est. receive". Users reconcile wallets and come up short by an amount never mentioned. Client cross equity has the same omission. | `market.ts:294-328`; `format.ts:146-157`; `contracts/market/src/lib.rs:2000-2027`; `PositionsList.tsx:288-305,812-816`; `CrossMarginBanner.tsx:39-47` | Compute pending funding client-side from the global cumulative rate (already fetched, `page.tsx:302-309`) + entry funding; add a Funding cell and an "Est. receive after funding" line in the close modal. |
| High | **Trade History "Fees" is always -$0.00 and Net PnL equals Gross**: the parser reads event field index 8 (`funding_paid`) which the deployed 8-field `position_closed` event does not emit; `undefined` coerces to 0. The venue positively asserts it charged nothing, then the wallet disagrees. ### Portfolio (/portfolio)

**Purpose & flow.** Read-only account dashboard for the connected wallet: net worth and margin stats, cumulative PnL chart with timeframe chips, collateral allocation, and trade history with stellar.expert deep links. There are zero write actions on the page — deposits, withdrawals, closes, and SL/TP all require a context switch to /trade — and the on-page connect card has no button (the CTA lives in the header).

**Strengths**

- The documented "USDC balance always shows 0" bug is fixed: the page consumes `useWallet().usdcBalance` populated on restore and connect (`web/app/portfolio/page.tsx:13`; `WalletProvider.tsx:101-107`; `useWallet.ts:59-64`; commit b709cbd).
- Every history row deep-links its tx hash to stellar.expert (`PortfolioHistory.tsx:38-40,160-168`) — right instinct for this audience.
- PnL chart bucketing is thoughtfully engineered: carries in cumulative PnL from before the window so 1D/1W/1M start at the correct baseline (`PnlChart.tsx:78-103`).
- Sensible polling discipline (60s refresh, per-asset deduped price fetches, `page.tsx:39-49,96-105`); tables wrap in `overflow-x-auto` and grids collapse sensibly; clean on-brand hierarchy with gold reserved for active states.

| Severity | Issue | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| Critical | **Net Worth silently excludes the cross-margin pool balance** — the page never calls `getCrossMarginBalance` (which /trade's banner uses), so depositing 500 USDC to cross margin makes Net Worth drop by 500. Pending lazy funding is also excluded, so equity is doubly off vs settlement. | `AccountHealth.tsx:21-24`; `page.tsx:113-118`; `market.ts:1140` (reader exists); `CrossMarginBanner.tsx:26,43-47` | Fetch cross balance alongside positions, add it to netWorth, and show it as its own reconcilable line, mirroring CrossMarginBanner's proven formula. |
| Critical | **A failed oracle read silently prices positions at $0**, rendering -100%-of-size losses and a collapsed Net Worth — `priceMap[p.asset] || 0` flows into PnL math with zero error indication; the exact scenario that makes a trader panic-close. | `page.tsx:44-53`; `format.ts:146-157`; `oracle.ts:44-47` | Treat missing price as unknown: render "—", keep last-good price with a stale badge, surface a retryable banner. Never let 0 into money math. |
| High | **PnL chart advertises 1M/All over a ~100-tx (or ~14h) data window** — Horizon `limit(100)` per account (every faucet claim eats the budget) with a 10,000-ledger fallback; older trades silently vanish and all-time PnL quietly rewrites itself. (Same root window as the trade-history truncation above.) | `market.ts:360-365,536-537`; `PnlChart.tsx:8` | Serve history from indexer projections via the gateway; until then cap timeframe options to actual coverage and caveat "recent trades only". |
| High | **No positions table and zero account actions — a dead-end dashboard**: positions are aggregated into stats but never listed (no entry/liq/leverage/margin mode visible); `onDeposit`/`onWithdraw` props exist but are never wired; every intent requires /trade. | `page.tsx:107-134`; `AccountHealth.tsx:11-12`; only depositCrossMargin call site is OrderPanel | Reuse /trade's PositionsList as an "Open Positions" row; wire Deposit/Withdraw on the Net Worth card to existing cross-margin flows; link Buying Power to /faucet at 0. |
| High | **Dead Filter/Export buttons and a permanently empty Transfers tab**: both buttons have no onClick; the tab is fed `transfers={[]}` so "No transfers yet" shows forever even for users with on-chain movements. Three fake affordances on the flagship account page. | `PortfolioHistory.tsx:66-73,177-179`; `page.tsx:129` | Hide Filter/Export until implemented (CSV export of the in-memory array is a ~20-line win); populate Transfers from indexer events or remove the tab. |
| Medium | **Invented risk metrics where real ones exist**: "Margin Usage Safe/Moderate/High" (collateral/netWorth with arbitrary 30/60% labels — a position one tick from liquidation can read "Safe") and "Collateral Health: Excellent / well-diversified" (meaningless on an all-USDC venue, robo-advisor copy). The genuine metric (equity vs maintenance margin) already exists on /trade. | `AccountHealth.tsx:27-28,124-143,112-121`; `AssetAllocation.tsx:67-69,152-164`; real formula `CrossMarginBanner.tsx:43-47` | Replace both with the CrossMarginBanner formulas and thresholds so /trade and /portfolio tell one story; rename Buying Power to "Available Balance". |
| Medium | **"this month" change is not monthly — the code admits it's a mock**: unrealized PnL over (netWorth − PnL), an instantaneous ratio with no time dimension. | `AccountHealth.tsx:30-32,65-78` | Relabel to "open PnL %" until indexer-backed equity snapshots exist. |
| Medium | **Stats flash wrong values on every load** — `isLoadingPositions` is computed but never passed down; Net Worth first paints wallet-only/$0.00 then jumps when positions/prices land (long window due to N+1 fetches). Skeleton component exists unused. | `page.tsx:17,21-62`; `market.ts:238-253`; `web/components/ui/Skeleton.tsx` | Pass the flag down and skeleton the four stat values until first resolve; keep last-known values on refreshes. |
| Medium | **No error surface anywhere**: both fetchers swallow to console; the 60s interval refreshes positions only (never trades or balances); an RPC outage looks identical to an empty account. | `page.tsx:57-58,76-77,96-105`; `WalletProvider.tsx:77-86` (refreshBalance unused) | Lightweight per-section error banner with retry; include trades + balance in the interval; add a "last updated Xs ago" stamp. |
| Medium | **Asset Allocation frames USDC margin as holding BTC/ETH/XLM**, and XLM's slice color is the brand gold `#eab308` — the allocation bar reads like a UI highlight rather than data. | `AssetAllocation.tsx:14-19,36-53,127` | Rename rows "BTC-PERP margin", panel "Margin Allocation"; give XLM a distinct dataviz hue that never collides with interaction gold. |
| Medium | **Date formats disagree on one page**: chart axis prints `getDate()/getMonth()+1` (European D/M) two rows above an en-US M/D/Y table — the same instant renders as 4/7 and 07/04. ~20 hand-rolled formats bypass the shared helpers. | `PnlChart.tsx:22-34`; `PortfolioHistory.tsx:27-36`; formatUSD unused in components/portfolio | One explicit unambiguous format app-wide ("04 Jul 2026, 14:22") via a shared date helper; route money through formatUSD. |
| Medium | **Disconnected/fresh-wallet experience is a dead end over fabricated zeros**: the connect card has no button; the PnL chart draws a flat line over ~30 invented dates with "+$0.00 (0.00%)"; empty panels offer no Faucet/Trade next step. | `AccountHealth.tsx:35-50`; `PnlChart.tsx:40-57`; `PortfolioHistory.tsx:88-91` | Put a ConnectButton in the card, dim zeroed panels when disconnected, give fresh wallets a Faucet → Trade CTA pair. |
| Low | **Redundant nested WalletProvider re-runs session restore and doubles balance fetches** — the page wraps itself although the root Providers already does (same pattern on /vault and /leaderboard; known issue #5). | `page.tsx:138-144`; `providers.tsx:35-40`; `layout.tsx:51` | Delete the page-level wrappers (pure deletion, three pages). |
| Low | **Chart polish gaps**: no crosshair/tooltip (comment admits it), 2-value y-axis overlaying the plot, "100.00%" fabricated when the window starts at 0, header row overflows near 360px, and `[TradeHistory]` debug logs fire on every visit. | `PnlChart.tsx:236-238,224-234,120-122,151-191`; `market.ts:354-355,367,384,539` | Pointer-tracked tooltip from existing points[]; "—" instead of fake 100%; flex-wrap the header; strip debug logging. |

### Vault — protocol LP pool (/vault)

**Purpose & flow.** The Earn side of the exchange: deposit USDC into the pool that is counterparty to all trader PnL, receive NOE LP tokens priced off pool AUM, monitor the position, redeem back to USDC. It is the destination of the landing "Deposit Now" CTA — yet it is absent from the app nav and reachable in-app only via a promo card inside /vaults (see Global chrome for the IA fix). Withdrawal is a two-signature flow (approve NOE, then withdraw) taking up to ~40s.

**Strengths**

- The notorious hardcoded 12.5% APY is actually fixed: APR renders an honest em-dash with "Shown once fee revenue is tracked" and Est. Daily Earnings is suppressed rather than faked (`StatsBar.tsx:46-54`; `page.tsx:46,103`; `YourPosition.tsx:51,89`).
- TVL is a real on-chain read of the vault's USDC balance (`vault.ts:95-122`) and the deposit preview math exactly mirrors the contract formula (`page.tsx:186` vs `contracts/vault/src/lib.rs:162-172`).
- Purpose-built skeletons for all four cards; solid double-submit protection; trustline friction proactively detected via Horizon with a one-click fix (`TrustlineWarning.tsx:13-25`).
- History rows deep-link to stellar.expert, dedupe by hash, paginate (`TransactionHistory.tsx:148-152,100-102,214-223`); all formatting pinned en-US (`format.ts:6-13,41-46,103-113`).
- The contract is more protective than the page lets on: withdrawals can never strand open-position payouts (M-4 gate, `contracts/vault/src/lib.rs:265-269`) and a per-account deposit cap exists (`lib.rs:155-160`).

| Severity | Issue | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| Critical | **Every money action fails and succeeds silently** — deposit, withdraw, and trustline handlers catch to `console.error` only; a failed deposit just stops the spinner, and the one number an LP cares about ("NOE received") is console.logged and discarded. Everything needed already exists in-repo: decoded vault errors 40/42/43, thrown human-readable simulation errors, and react-hot-toast live on the sibling leader-vault modal. This is the only money surface without feedback. | `page.tsx:109-113,131-135,146,150-153,166-181`; `contractErrors.ts:26-29`; `client.ts:56-60`; contrast `DepositWithdrawModal.tsx:145-169` | Wrap all three handlers with `toast.error(decodeContractError(err))` + success toasts showing actual amounts ("Deposited 100 USDC → 99.7 NOE"); reuse the leader-vault modal pattern verbatim. |
| Critical | **LP counterparty risk effectively undisclosed at the point of deposit** — the only mention is one text-xs line at 50% opacity inside a collapsed accordion; a purpose-built RiskDisclosure component exists and is rendered nowhere; funnel copy is upside-only ("the simplest way to earn"). A user can deposit without ever seeing principal is at risk. | `HowItWorks.tsx:8,49-51`; `RiskDisclosure.tsx:3-20` (only referenced by `vault/index.ts:7`); `web/app/vaults/page.tsx:90-92`; `YourPosition.tsx:110-112` | Render the existing RiskDisclosure beside the deposit card; add one plain sentence under the deposit button ("Pool value falls when traders profit — principal at risk"); expand HowItWorks by default for first-timers. |
| High | **Disconnected visitors see fabricated pool stats — TVL $0.00, NOE $1.000** — all reads require a publicKey and early-return without a wallet, so the landing-Earn funnel shows an empty/dead pool as if real (no skeleton because `isLoading && isConnected`). `getNoePrice` also silently returns a fabricated $1.00 on any RPC failure even when connected. | `page.tsx:62-65,38-48,200-205`; `vault.ts:95-122,158-183` (fallback :179-182); `StatsBar.tsx:26-65` | Fetch TVL/price with the existing null-pubkey simulation pattern; show "—" + retry on failure, never a fabricated $1.00. |
| High | **Withdraw springs two unannounced wallet signatures behind one button** — approve NOE (popup #1) then withdraw (popup #2), 20–40s total, no step indicator, narration only in console.log; rejecting popup #2 leaves a paid approval accomplishing nothing with zero explanation. | `page.tsx:158-182`; `vault.ts:34-64,70-89`; `client.ts:104-112` | Two-step progress state ("1/2 Approving NOE… 2/2 Withdrawing…"), pre-announce the double signature, toast between steps; consider a persistent max allowance for single-signature repeats. |
| High | **Deposit is not gated on the NOE trustline** — the disabled condition omits `hasTrustline`, so a first-timer's very first deposit is a guaranteed simulation failure that (per the silent-error issue) surfaces nowhere; the TrustlineWarning renders in the neighboring card. | `DepositWithdrawCard.tsx:181`; `page.tsx:210-241`; `contracts/vault/src/lib.rs:194-195` | When `!hasTrustline`, replace the deposit button with the inline "Add NOE to Wallet" action (combined flow) or hard-disable with an explanatory line. |
| Medium | **Availability claims contradict the contract**: "Convert NOE back to USDC anytime" and the /vaults funnel's "Withdrawals: Instant" vs a contract that can refuse (pause, reserved payouts, liquidity) plus the 2-signature ~40s flow; no "available to withdraw now" figure; the deposit cap is never surfaced pre-submit. | `HowItWorks.tsx:45`; `web/app/vaults/page.tsx:108-111`; `contracts/vault/src/lib.rs:224-226,256-269,155-160` | Soften copy; display available withdrawal liquidity (vault balance minus reserved payouts, both readable on-chain); validate the cap client-side. |
| Medium | **Stale revenue copy: "0.1% fee on every trade"** — the deprecated legacy fee; real fees are tiered 0.02%/0.05%. It is the page's only quantitative earnings claim, overestimating LP fee income 2–5×. | `HowItWorks.tsx:40`; `contracts/market/src/lib.rs:1940,1969` | "maker/taker fees (0.02%/0.05%, volume-tiered) from every trade + trader losses" or drop the number. |
| Medium | **Pool Share computed apples-to-oranges and rendered with a spurious '+'**: AUM-based numerator over raw-token-balance denominator (wrong whenever traders hold unrealized PnL), then formatted with a signed percent so a share reads "+2.00%" like a delta. The correct input (circulating supply from `get_pool_info`) is fetched and thrown away. | `YourPosition.tsx:49-50,85`; `format.ts:51-54`; `page.tsx:69-106` | Compute share as NOE balance / circulating NOE; format unsigned. |
| Medium | **Deposit "Max" fills 95% (floored) while the label shows the full balance** — an unexplained mismatch (gas is XLM, not USDC), and the withdraw tab's Max uses 100%, so the two tabs behave differently. A Max that lies erodes trust in every other number. | `DepositWithdrawCard.tsx:128-131` vs `199-205` | Make Max fill exactly the displayed balance, or label the buffer ("Max (keeps 5% buffer)"). |
| Medium | **Disconnected users get a disabled "Connect Wallet" button that does nothing** — same dead-CTA trap as the OrderPanel, on both tabs. | `DepositWithdrawCard.tsx:181,190,260,269` | Make it live and open the wallet modal (machinery is already on the page). |
| Medium | **`get_pool_info` gating blanks real data**: `setPoolStats` sits inside `if (poolInfo)`, so one failed call throws away the successfully fetched price/balance/TVL; the parse helpers for it are dead code. On flaky testnet RPC this is a real "my position vanished" scenario. | `page.tsx:69-106` (gate :82, dead helpers :89-94) | Set stats from whichever reads succeeded; use poolInfo for circulating supply or drop the call; surface partial-failure state. |
| Low | **Every deposit/withdraw flashes the whole page back to skeletons** — post-tx refresh sets `isLoading(true)`, dissolving all four cards at the moment of highest anxiety; combined with no success toast, the only confirmation is numbers quietly changing after the flash. | `page.tsx:67,148-149,175-176,204,216,240` | Separate isInitialLoad from isRefreshing; update values in place on refetch. |
| Low | **0.3% deposit/withdraw fee hardcoded in the UI** — literal 0.997/0.003 and "Fee (0.3%)"; matches today's deploy but the contract exposes getters and the admin can change fees up to 10%, after which the UI silently misquotes "You Receive". | `page.tsx:186-191`; `DepositWithdrawCard.tsx:173,252`; `contracts/vault/src/lib.rs:580-587,611-635`; `scripts/deploy_staging.sh:163` | Read `get_deposit_fee`/`get_withdraw_fee` once in fetchPoolData; derive preview and label. |
| Low | **History frames LP actions as raw token transfers** ("Received NOE"/"Sent NOE") with no USDC leg or fee, uses raw console.log (3 sites) instead of the gated debugLog, and truncates at 100 effects. | `TransactionHistory.tsx:63,65,81,127,192-193`; `debug.ts:6-9` | Pair NOE movement with same-tx USDC to label rows "Deposited 100 USDC → 99.7 NOE"; switch to debugLog. |
| Low | **No page heading or framing; copy/color inconsistencies**: page opens on stat tiles with no h1; HowItWorks is a fixed `grid-cols-3` that crushes at 320–375px; withdraw labels NOE "You Pay" (users redeem); the primary deposit CTA is green while green means "long" on /trade and the brand's action color is gold. | `page.tsx:197-205`; `HowItWorks.tsx:31`; `DepositWithdrawCard.tsx:198,186,100`; `web/app/vaults/page.tsx:117` | Add h1 + one-sentence subtitle ("Provide USDC liquidity, earn trading fees, bear trader PnL"); `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3`; rename "You Redeem"; gold primary CTA. |

### Vaults marketplace & detail (/vaults, /vaults/[id])

**Purpose & flow.** Marketplace for browsing and backing user-created leader trading vaults (deposit USDC, share the leader's PnL, leader keeps 10% above high-water mark), plus a per-vault detail page for deposits/withdrawals and leader evaluation; a hero card funnels newcomers to the protocol LP vault. Deposit is 4 clicks + sign via a modal; creating a vault currently dead-ends (list never updates without a manual reload); evaluating a leader requires opening stellar.expert per trade because the history table lacks asset/direction/leverage/size columns.

**Strengths**

- Chain-first marketplace architecture: the grid reads `view_vault` directly from Soroban and overlays API aggregates best-effort, degrading to em-dash tiles when the gateway is down (`web/app/vaults/page.tsx:13-21,37-43`; `vaultFactory.ts:238-244`) — deliberately resilient, rare in dApps.
- Excellent progressive-disclosure IA: the protocol vault is the hero/default and leader vaults are framed "Advanced: follow a specific trader", with the empty state routing newcomers back to the simple product (`page.tsx:81-137,154-165`).
- Honest mechanic disclosure where it exists: live `profitShareBps` and the 5% leader-skin rule on detail (`[id]/page.tsx:139-149`); the Leader Holding tile turns red below 5% (`VaultMetrics.tsx:121-126`); CreateVaultModal states the 10% share and seed-first rule up front (`CreateVaultModal.tsx:62-67`).
- Deposit modal preflight quality: authoritative on-chain balance + `shares_of` reads, Max button, previews mirroring contract formulas, busy-flag double-submit protection (`DepositWithdrawModal.tsx:82-137,154-181,301-303`); simulation-before-sign surfaces most rejections before the wallet popup (`client.ts:56-60`).
- Verifiability as a trust primitive: every activity/trade row and the leader address link to stellar.expert (`VaultActivity.tsx:75-85`; `VaultTradeHistory.tsx:93-102`; `[id]/page.tsx:68-82`); the leader-mode handoff to /trade re-fetches fresh vault data (`VaultActions.tsx:22-31`; `trade/page.tsx:86-103`).

| Severity | Issue | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| Critical | **TVL/NAV/position value silently exclude capital deployed in open positions** — the contract resyncs `total_usdc` to liquid balance after every leader trade, so a leader opening an $80 position collapses a $100 vault's NAV to 0.2. The UI displays this as truth everywhere (card TVL/NAV, "Value at NAV", the withdraw preview): a depositor exiting mid-trade permanently forfeits their share of in-flight capital, MyVaultPosition paints the dip as red "Unrealised P&L" (encouraging the panic), and depositing at the depressed NAV mints cheap shares that dilute others. With ≥2 funded vaults the sync reads the WHOLE factory balance (known audit V-1, deferred to v1.1), cross-contaminating every number. Deposit buttons are live with zero caveat. | `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:584-589,332,358`; `MyVaultPosition.tsx:73-79,98`; `DepositWithdrawModal.tsx:132-135`; `indexer/src/vaultSync.ts:1-10`; `docs/AUDIT-2026-06.md:90`; `TASKS.md:208` | Until V-1 lands, compensate in UI: show "Deployed in positions: $X" as its own line, label NAV "liquid NAV", hard-warn in the withdraw tab whenever openPositions > 0, and banner the leader-vault surface as beta. |
| Critical | **APY can never be negative and is trivially inflatable** — `apyBps` is set only when lifetime PnL > 0 (losing vaults show 0.00%, indistinguishable from new; VaultCard's red branch is dead code), computed as lifetime PnL over CURRENT TVL annualized with days floored at 1 (a 1-day vault with one +2% trade shows ~730%; a leader can withdraw to shrink the denominator). This is the primary decision metric on a money-allocation page. | `api/src/services/vaults.ts:256-263`; `VaultCard.tsx:37-45,67`; `VaultMetrics.tsx:70-74` | Signed APY from time-weighted TVL (deposit/withdraw history exists); suppress annualization below a minimum age ("since inception: +2.1% (3d)"); methodology tooltip on the card, not desktop-only. |
| High | **Detail page has no chain fallback, no loading.tsx, no error.tsx** — the marketplace survives gateway outages but clicking any card lands on a force-dynamic server page fetching everything from the gateway: outage → unbranded Next "Application error"; stale indexer (the documented D-4 incident pattern) → 404 for a vault the user just saw; fresh vaults 404 during the indexing window; and the click gives zero feedback while five API round-trips run. | `[id]/page.tsx:19-38`; `web/lib/api/vaults.ts:32-38`; find: no error.tsx/loading.tsx under web/app; contrast `vaults/page.tsx:37-43` | Add loading.tsx + error.tsx for the route; fall back to `getVaultInfo()` on-chain when the gateway 404s/fails (the `vaultRowFromOnChain` adapter exists), rendering history sections as "unavailable". |
| High | **Creating a vault is a dead-end** — `router.refresh()` cannot re-run the client `useEffect([])` fetch, so the toast says success while the grid is unchanged; after a hard reload the vault appears at the BOTTOM of the oldest-first grid and can still 404 until indexed. | `CreateVaultModal.tsx:33-37`; `vaults/page.tsx:22-73`; `vaultFactory.ts:238-244` | Read the new vault id from the tx result and route to /vaults/[id] with an "indexing…" placeholder, or optimistically prepend a card. |
| High | **No sorting, filtering, or search; vaults render oldest-first** — the grid is `listAllVaultsOnChain` verbatim (id 0..N-1); no TVL/APY/drawdown/depositors sort, no paused filter, no leader search. Fails the core browsing job vs the Hyperliquid vault-marketplace baseline as soon as >~6 vaults exist. | `vaults/page.tsx:167-172,22-73`; `vaultFactory.ts:238-244` | Client-side sort dropdown (TVL desc default), paused filter, name/leader text filter — all data already in memory; consider a table-view toggle for pros. |
| High | **Withdraw failure handling is wrong twice**: the "insufficient liquidity" pre-check is mathematically dead (requires shares > circulating, but exceedsBalance fires first), so the one message explaining "leader has capital deployed" never renders; and `humanizeError` maps FactoryError #10 (InsufficientBalance — exactly what a withdraw hits) to a message about deposits, while #9 (InsufficientShares) claims a USDC-allowance problem that cannot exist (deposits use authorized transfer). | `DepositWithdrawModal.tsx:113-118,47-66`; `contracts/vault_factory/src/types.rs:85-86`; `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:137-145` | Fix mappings to the FactoryError enum, make messages mode-aware, and replace the dead check with a real one (requested usdc_out vs liquid USDC; warn when openPositions > 0). |
| High | **A failed balance read silently bricks deposits with a false "Amount exceeds USDC balance"** — fetch failure goes to console.warn, `usdcBalance` stays 0n, every positive amount trips exceedsBalance and the submit disables; Max shows "(0.0000)" during load. No loading/failed/zero distinction. | `DepositWithdrawModal.tsx:73-74,97-101,111-112,175-181,243` | Track balance as loading/loaded/error; skip the gate while not loaded (chain + simulation enforce it anyway) or show "Balance unavailable — retry". |
| Medium | **Post-transaction the page shows stale data with no pending affordance** — modal closes and `router.refresh()` re-reads a gateway whose projection lags (2s indexer poll + 10s aggregate TTL), so TVL/Your Position typically still show pre-tx values; after signing + confirming, "nothing changed" reads as a failed transaction. | `VaultActions.tsx:38`; `api/src/services/vaults.ts:68`; `indexer/src/config.ts:30`; `DepositWithdrawModal.tsx:163-166` | Optimistically update from the tx result (minted shares are known) or keep the modal open with "confirmed — syncing…" polling until the API row advances. |
| Medium | **No risk disclosure for leader-vault depositors; a leader pause freezes withdrawals with no explanation** — nothing states principal risk, that pause blocks BOTH deposit and withdraw (funds freeze at leader discretion), or the NAV mechanics; the orphaned RiskDisclosure component is rendered nowhere; when paused, the only signal is a disabled button. The adjacent hero's "Withdrawals: Instant" sharpens the unwarned contrast. | `RiskDisclosure.tsx` (unused); `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:128-130,196-198`; `VaultActions.tsx:19-21`; `vaults/page.tsx:108-111` | Compact risk card on detail (principal risk, pause-freeze, deployment mechanics) + one acknowledgment line in the deposit tab; on pause, say who paused and what resumes when. |
| Medium | **"Your Position" derives shares and "Unrealised P&L" from truncated event lists** — sums the newest ≤200 deposits/withdraws (silently wrong past 200; can show "No position yet" to a real depositor), and the "Unrealised P&L" number is actually lifetime net including realized flows. The authoritative `shares_of` reader exists and is used by the modal, so page and modal can disagree about the same wallet. | `MyVaultPosition.tsx:21-31,51-57,79,98`; `[id]/page.tsx:33-38`; `vaultFactory.ts:161-168`; `api/src/services/vaults.ts:67` | Read shares via `shares_of` on the client; keep events only for flow counters; relabel "Net P&L (est.)" or split realized vs unrealized. |
| Medium | **Leader trade history omits asset, direction, leverage, and size** — the exact columns needed to judge a leader; the only recourse is stellar.expert per row, though each leader_open tx also emits `position_opened` (asset/direction/size/entry) which the indexer already decodes. | `VaultTradeHistory.tsx:47-55`; `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:335-338`; CLAUDE.md event formats | Join vault_trades with same-tx position events in the indexer (as done for pnl in migration 011); add Asset/Side/Size/Entry columns. |
| Medium | **Drawdown mislabeled "Max Drawdown" and anchored to a claim-reset HWM** — the formula is current-vs-HWM floored at 0: a vault that crashed 80% and recovered shows 0.00%; a never-claiming leader's HWM hides true peak-to-trough; and NAV dips from mere capital deployment read as "drawdown". | `api/src/services/vaults.ts:250-252`; `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:359`; `VaultMetrics.tsx:75-80`; `VaultPnlSummary.tsx:129` | Compute true max drawdown from the stored NAV/PnL series, or relabel honestly "Current vs HWM" with a tooltip until then. |

| Low | **Hardcoded protocol-vault hero stats**: "NOE Price $1.000" as a literal (a live reader exists), "APR Variable", "Withdrawals Instant" — a wrong price on the flagship card undermines the whole surface's credibility. | `vaults/page.tsx:97-111` | Fetch the live NOE price (30s cache) or drop the tile; "Instant" → "Anytime". |

### Vault leader manage (/vaults/[id]/manage) + leader mode on /trade

**Purpose & flow.** The console for a vault leader: trade pooled follower USDC through factory proxies, monitor metrics, and claim the profit share (10% of NAV gain above HWM). Two divergent leader surfaces now exist: the newer, well-built leader mode on /trade (reached via "Trade as leader →") and the legacy manage page — which is linked from nowhere, renders without the site Header, and is the ONLY place the economically-critical Claim button lives. All money numbers on this surface inherit the V-1 factory-wide accounting corruption documented in the previous section.

**Strengths**

- /trade leader mode is genuinely well designed: persistent amber "Leader mode" banner with vault name + live balance, an "Exit leader mode" escape hatch, unsupported order types elided rather than left broken (`OrderPanel.tsx:64-71,571-595,691-718`); balance-aware validation with dedicated copy and a deep link that refetches fresh vault data (`trade/page.tsx:86-103`; `OrderPanel.tsx:226-231`).
- Leader position fetch avoids the N+1 whole-market scan via the indexer hint (`trade/page.tsx:140-151`); close has a real confirmation modal with pending state (`PositionsList.tsx:113-126,279-323`); SL/TP buttons are correctly hidden in leader mode instead of failing wallet auth (`trade/page.tsx:512-517`).
- The manage page's Reminders card is honest plain-language risk copy (accounting resync, 5% skin rule, HWM mechanics — `manage/page.tsx:38-50`); VaultMetrics shows real data with em-dash fallbacks, no invented numbers (`VaultMetrics.tsx:36-41,63-127`); follower transparency on the detail page is strong.

| Severity | Issue | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| Critical | **Leader-mode positions list is factory-wide: shows — and lets a leader close — other vaults' positions.** All vaults share the factory contract as trader; with 2+ active vaults, vault A's leader sees vault B's positions as their own, and `leader_close_position(vaultA.id, positionId)` succeeds because the contract never checks position membership — settling another vault's PnL into vault A's books. Cross-vault interference from the UI. | `trade/page.tsx:146-151`; `web/lib/api/positions.ts:20-30`; `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:344-368` (no membership check) | Filter the leader positions list to the selected vault via the indexer's positionId↔vaultId mapping (`web/types/vault.ts:37-49`) and tag rows; add a contract-side membership check as the hard guarantee. |
| High | **The manage page is orphaned and headerless** — grep confirms zero links to the route anywhere; the leader CTA goes to /trade?vault= instead; the page renders no `<Header/>` so a bookmark visit has no navigation and no wallet-connect (its own gate says "Connect your wallet" with no affordance). An abandoned v1 surface (built 2026-05-07) left reachable, carrying the only Claim entry point. | `manage/page.tsx:1-27,21-54`; `LeaderManageGate.tsx:11-19`; `VaultActions.tsx:22-31`; git log d0942f1 | Either redirect the route to /trade?vault={id}, or rebuild it as the true leader console (positions, claimable amount, pause control) linked from the detail header — with the standard Header in all cases. |
| High | **The profit-share Claim is only reachable by typing an unlinked URL**, shows no claimable amount (every input to the on-chain formula is already client-side), and claiming zero produces a cryptic raw revert ("Claim failed: Error(Contract, #15)"). | `LeaderTradePanel.tsx:89-100,192-203`; `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:466-490`; `web/types/vault.ts:5-35` | Surface "Claim profit share — $X claimable" as a leader-only button on the vault detail page; disable at zero; show expected payout in the pending toast. |
| High | **LeaderTradePanel opens positions with follower money completely blind** — no mark price, size/liq/fee preview, vault-balance display or max check, no 10-USDC minimum check, no paused-vault warning (`vault.paused` is available but never passed). Less information than the personal trade form provides for one's own money. | `LeaderTradePanel.tsx:55-71,102-167`; `LeaderManageGate.tsx:37`; contrast `OrderPanel.tsx:217-243,1138-1217` | Kill the duplicate panel in favour of /trade leader mode, or bring it to parity (balance + presets, order summary, min/max validation, paused banner). |
| High | **Closing on the manage page requires hand-typing a numeric position id the UI never shows** — the only place ids appear is the detail page's mixed open/close event log; a mistyped id targets a DIFFERENT factory position (no vault scoping, above) and closes it at market with no confirmation step. | `LeaderTradePanel.tsx:73-87,173-189`; `VaultTradeHistory.tsx:49-84` | Replace the id input with a rendered list of this vault's open positions with per-row Close + the /trade confirmation modal. |
| High | **Factory contract errors decode into wrong messages in leader mode; the manage page doesn't decode at all** — `decodeContractError` uses the market enum whose codes collide with FactoryError: NotLeader (#6) surfaces as "arithmetic overflow", InvalidName (#4) as "Trading is currently paused"; the common LeaderMinimumViolated (#11)/Paused (#12)/NoFeesToClaim (#15) fall through raw. The manage panel shows raw strings sliced to 200 chars. | `contractErrors.ts:5-56` vs `contracts/vault_factory/src/types.rs:76-92`; `OrderPanel.tsx:385`; `LeaderTradePanel.tsx:66-67,82-83,95-96` | Add a FactoryError table selected by target contract; route LeaderTradePanel through the same decoder. |
| Medium | **No follower-funds guardrail on leader opens** — % presets operate on the whole vault balance and submit fires immediately; closes get a confirmation modal, opens with pooled money get none. A slipped "100%" commits the entire follower pool to a leveraged position. | `OrderPanel.tsx:512-515,1232-1253`; modal exists at `PositionsList.tsx:279-323` | Leader-mode-only confirm step (or inline warning) showing % of vault consumed and resulting liq price; keep personal mode one-click. |
| Medium | **Manage-page metrics go stale immediately after the leader's own action** — server-rendered once; success handlers only toast and clear the input (no `router.refresh()`), so TVL/positions tiles contradict the chain until manual reload. The detail-page deposit flow gets this right. | `manage/page.tsx:8-19,36`; `LeaderTradePanel.tsx:63-64,79,93`; contrast `VaultActions.tsx:38` | `router.refresh()` (delayed ~2s) in each success handler. |
| Medium | **One shared busy flag makes all three action buttons read "Signing…" at once**, with no toast/progress during the 5–30s confirmation — ambiguous feedback exactly when trust matters. | `LeaderTradePanel.tsx:43,58,76,90,164-166,186-188,199-201` | Per-action pending labels ("Opening…", "Closing #42…", "Claiming…") + toast.promise like the /trade leader flow. |
| Medium | **Leader-mode tab inconsistency on /trade**: Positions shows the vault, but Orders and Trade History silently show the leader's PERSONAL account with no label — vault activity and personal history conflate for leaders and watching followers. | `trade/page.tsx:146-151` vs `221-229,541`; `TradeHistory.tsx:244-258` | Scope Orders/History to the vault (indexer vault_trades has it) or overlay an explicit "Personal wallet data" notice. |
| Medium | **30s confirmation timeout reports failure for txs that may still succeed** — "Transaction did not complete: NOT_FOUND" after 30×1s polls; a leader retries and can open duplicate leveraged positions of follower money. Both leader paths share this code. | `client.ts:77-130` (throw at :130); via `vaultFactory.ts:28-41` | On timeout surface "still pending" with tx hash + explorer link and keep polling; disable resubmit until the hash resolves. |
| Medium | **Contract supports leader limit orders and cancels but no surface exposes them — and a code comment denies they exist**: `leader_place_limit_order`/`leader_cancel_order` ship in the contract; the web wrapper implements only open/close/claim; OrderPanel's comment asserting they don't exist will steer future work away. | `contracts/vault_factory/src/lib.rs:371,437`; `vaultFactory.ts` (no wrappers); `OrderPanel.tsx:64-66,691-694` | Wrap the two contract fns and enable the Limit tab in leader mode; at minimum correct the comment. |
| Low | **Manage copy hardcodes "10%"/"5%" while `profitShareBps` is per-vault** — the page where the leader claims compensation can misstate it; the detail page renders the field dynamically. | `manage/page.tsx:44-47` vs `[id]/page.tsx:142`; `web/types/vault.ts:15` | Interpolate the live bps into the copy. |
| Low | **Wallet-gate hydration flash and duplicated gate copy** — the persisted store is empty on first render so a connected leader briefly sees the connect prompt; LeaderTradePanel repeats a second, slightly different gate that is dead code. | `LeaderManageGate.tsx:11-19`; `LeaderTradePanel.tsx:45-53`; `walletStore.ts:25-27` | Gate on store hydration (neutral skeleton until rehydrated); drop the inner gate. |

### Referrals (/referrals) + site-wide ?ref banner

**Purpose & flow.** Traders register a unique on-chain referral code, share a ?ref link, and monitor referees/earnings; from v1.1 they claim a 10% USDC share of referee fees while referees get 4% off. A floating banner mounted in the root layout captures `?ref=CODE` on any page and lets the visitor bind via a signed `set_referrer` transaction. An "Advanced" disclosure exchanges a signed wallet challenge for gateway analytics — with a memory-only session that re-mints a permanent API key on every refresh.

**Strengths**

- Honest money copy: every earning number is consistently labeled "from v1.1" — no fabricated earnings on a surface where fake numbers are the industry norm; the 10%/4% figures verified against contract defaults (`contracts/referral/src/types.rs:6-9`).
- Real-time on-chain availability check via `resolve_code` simulation with clear inline states and a pre-submit re-check for races (`CreateCodeCard.tsx:183-210,79-84`; `web/lib/stellar/referral.ts:84-91`).
- Referral contract errors #8–#12 mapped to plain English in both create and bind flows (`CreateCodeCard.tsx:13-21`; `ReferralBanner.tsx:84-86`); simulation-first building surfaces failures before the wallet popup.
- Well-engineered banner capture: format-validated one-shot URL parse, `replaceState` strip so re-shares don't re-trigger, localStorage persistence, lazy-loaded stellar-sdk (`lib/referralCode.ts:22-38`; `ReferralBanner.tsx:69-72`).
- Public-by-default architecture: code creation, stats, and lookups need no API key; the closed-beta 403 renders as a calm inline notice with contact paths (`ReferralSignIn.tsx:88-90,164-196`); every trade/claim row links to stellar.expert (`ReferralActivity.tsx:54-62`).

| Severity | Issue | Evidence | Fix direction |
|---|---|---|---|
| High | **Page copy promises auto-binding "on their first authed call" but binding is an explicit, easily-missed banner action** — the only `set_referrer` call site is the banner's button (grep-confirmed); no auto-bind exists in web or gateway. Referrers share links believing attribution is passive, then watch referredCount stay 0 — a direct trust hit on the attribution surface. | `referrals/page.tsx:26-27,58-59`; `CreateCodeCard.tsx:115-118`; `ShareLinkCard.tsx:33-37` vs `ReferralBanner.tsx:62-91`; `referral.ts:44-52` | Rewrite the three copy instances to the real mechanic ("they accept with one signed transaction"), or make it true: auto-prompt bind on first wallet connect — `consumePendingReferral()` (`lib/referralCode.ts:50`) was built for exactly this and is dead code. |
| High | **Successful registration appears to vanish** — after `create_code` confirms, the UI immediately re-reads the eventually-consistent indexer (2s poll + ~5s ledger + Turso write), almost always misses, and shows the empty register form again as if it failed; re-submitting yields "You already have a code registered." A stale indexer (the D-4 incident) shows the create form indefinitely. | `ReferralSignIn.tsx:42-69,132`; `CreateCodeCard.tsx:85-89`; `api/src/services/referral.ts:132`; `indexer/src/config.ts:30` | Optimistically render the just-registered code, poll with backoff ~30s, or read the profile from chain via simulateView (as lookupCode does), keeping the indexer for activity tables only. |
| High | **Attribution is single-shot with no recovery**: dismissing the banner calls `clearPendingReferral()` — permanent, unconfirmed forfeit — and there is no manual code-entry field anywhere in the app for verbally-relayed codes. Every competitor offers one. Silently caps program conversion. | `ReferralBanner.tsx:54-60,52`; no code input on `referrals/page.tsx` | Add a "Have a referral code?" input on /referrals calling `setReferrer`; on dismiss, park the pending code behind a low-key entry point instead of deleting it. |
| Medium | **Banner's primary CTA is a dead button when disconnected** — "Connect wallet to bind" is `disabled={busy \|\| !wallet.address}` at the single most important conversion moment; the fresh referred visitor must discover the navbar ConnectButton alone. | `ReferralBanner.tsx:184-194` | Make it live: open the stellar-wallets-kit modal, then proceed to bind once connected. |
| Medium | **Every analytics sign-in mints a new permanent server-side API key; sessions are memory-only** — refresh drops the credentials while the key stays live; "Sign out" wipes local state without revoking. Regular dashboard users accumulate orphaned live keys visible in /api-keys. | `sessionAuthStore.ts:11-17`; `ReferralSignIn.tsx:76-85`; `keys.ts:86-98`; `ReferralDashboard.tsx:149-156` | Persist the session key (sessionStorage minimum), reuse an existing "referral-dashboard" key, revoke on sign-out via the existing endpoint. |
| Medium | **Silent API failure shows the register form to existing code-holders; the localhost fallback makes it the guaranteed prod behavior when the env var is missing** — `getReferralInfo` errors are swallowed and `self` stays null; `API_BASE` falls back to `http://localhost:4000`, so a Vercel deploy missing `NEXT_PUBLIC_NOETHER_API_URL` turns this on for every visitor and kills the banner lookup too. | `ReferralSignIn.tsx:56-64`; `web/lib/api/referral.ts:8`; `web/lib/api/keys.ts:6` | Distinguish "API unreachable" (degraded notice + retry) from "no code"; fail loudly when the env var is absent in production (see Faucet/API-Keys for the shared fix). |
| Medium | **Banner invites already-bound and self-referring users into guaranteed-fail binds** despite having the data to prevent it — no check of the visitor's existing binding, no self-code check, and the CTA stays active when the lookup positively 404'd. Click → wait → red toast for outcomes the UI could state upfront. | `ReferralBanner.tsx:38-50,62-91`; `web/lib/api/referral.ts:30-52` | On connect, fetch the visitor's binding: swap CTA for "already referred by CODE" / "this is your own code" / "code doesn't exist" as applicable. |
| Medium | **Code case-sensitivity is never communicated** — the contract stores codes case-sensitively; "NoeMerth" told verbally as "noemerth" fails with UnknownCode; "trader" and "Trader" can coexist as different referrers. Copy-link users are safe, hiding the trap. | `contracts/referral/src/lib.rs:66-67`; `CreateCodeCard.tsx:162-166`; `referralCode.ts:15` | Lowercase-normalize at the UI boundary (create, capture, bind) — or state "case-sensitive" in helper text and warn on mixed-case codes. |
| Medium | **The "from v1.1" explanations disappear on mobile** (`hidden md:block`), leaving three unexplained $0.00 stat cards — on the primary device context for share links, reading as a broken product. | `ReferralStats.tsx:81` | Keep hints visible on mobile, or one always-visible "accrual starts in v1.1" note above the stat row. |
| Medium | **A11y gaps at the two money moments**: the code input has no accessible name (placeholder only) with unassociated validity feedback; the banner claims `role="dialog"` but never receives focus, has no trap/Escape, and its arrival/success (1.8s auto-dismiss) is unannounced; the Advanced disclosure lacks `aria-expanded`. | `CreateCodeCard.tsx:169-182`; `ui/Input.tsx:17-21`; `ReferralBanner.tsx:105-107,76-80`; `ReferralSignIn.tsx:145-154` | Label the input + `aria-describedby`; make the banner a polite live region (`role="status"`) or a real dialog; add `aria-expanded`. |
| Low | **Two error decoders in series with conflicting tables**: `buildTransaction` runs referral errors through the market mapping first — CodeTooShort (#6) would render "arithmetic overflow", NotMarket (#4) "Trading is currently paused" — and CreateCodeCard's own humanize can never match #5–#7 because the pattern was rewritten upstream. Currently shielded by client validation; a latent trap. | `client.ts:58-59`; `contractErrors.ts:12-13`; `CreateCodeCard.tsx:13-21`; `contracts/referral/src/types.rs:65-81` | Make `decodeContractError` contract-aware (table per target contract) or stop decoding in buildTransaction. |
| Low | **Availability check simulates from the user's own account** — a brand-new unfunded wallet fails `getAccount`, degrading to "couldn't verify" and later raw account-not-found errors, though the null-pubkey convention exists for exactly this. | `referral.ts:63-78`; `CreateCodeCard.tsx:207-209` | Use the null public key for `resolve_code`; detect account-not-found on the write path and point to /faucet. |
| Low | **Sign-out is the only dashboard error recovery, and the render tree is duplicated** — a transient gateway hiccup forces discarding the session (and minting yet another key); the stats/claim/share stack is assembled twice with copy already drifting. | `ReferralDashboard.tsx:99-112,140-148` vs `ReferralSignIn.tsx:122-141` | Add a plain Retry that re-runs the fetch; extract one `<ReferralOverview>` composed by both paths. |
| Low | **Production-polish nits**: console.log of wallet addresses on every register attempt; a Turkish source comment; hardcoded `/explorer/testnet/` links; the Discord invite duplicated in two components; no route metadata (generic tab title). | `CreateCodeCard.tsx:77,136`; `referrals/page.tsx:40,1-8`; `ReferralActivity.tsx:55,104`; `ReferralSignIn.tsx:187` | Gate logging behind debugLog; derive explorer network from NETWORK config; centralize the Discord URL; add route metadata. |
